╭──────────────────── logging - explorer - system_message ─────────────────────╮
│                                                                              │
│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │
│    and command-line related tasks.                                           │
│  • You, all other agents and the user operate on the same system.            │
│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Explorer agent of the Multi Agent System.               │
│  • Based on the provided task by the user, use the given tools to gather     │
│    some basic information relevant to completing the task.                   │
│  • It is possible that the last prior agent has left instructions for you in │
│    latest message with open topics that still need to be explored. If so,    │
│    prioritize answering the other agents' exploration requests.              │
│  • Do not try to solve the tasks, instead, collect only basic information    │
│    that will assist later agents in planning and actively solving the task.  │
│  • When finished write a concise summary of only the gathered information    │
│    and include the tag '[subtaskplanner]'.                                   │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                         Code Execution Capabilities                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your are equipped with different tools for executing code.                │
│  • Do only use the tools to execute code. Code inside a Markdown Code Block  │
│    will not be executed.                                                     │
│  • Use Shell tool for simple, single bash commands. Do not use it for        │
│    commands that open interactive terminal programs (e.g. git log or man).   │
│  • Use Terminal tool to execute commands in a persistent terminal instance   │
│    and get back the exact terminal screen. This is especially useful for     │
│    interactive programs such as git log, man, or vim.                        │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                             More Operating Rules                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • Think and act step by step and explain your actions and reasoning.        │
│  • Break complex objectives into smaller sub-goals if necessary.             │
│  • Use tools to gather knowledge or verify assumptions before proceeding.    │
│  • If you call a tool it will be executed and results will be returned to    │
│    you.                                                                      │

│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │

│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │

│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Subtaskplanner agent of the Multi Agent System.         │
│  • Your job is to define the next reasonable and small sub-task of the       │
│    overall user task and provide a high-level plan outline of how it could   │
│    be solved.                                                                │
│  • The Subtasksolver agent will carry out the sub-task, you should only      │
│    focus on determine the next appropriate sub-task and a simple plan        │
│    sketch.                                                                   │
│  • Make sure the sub-task and plan sketch describe only the next reasonable  │
│    step forward, keeping in mind that some work may already have been        │
│    completed by previous agents.                                             │
│  • The sub-task should be described concisely in verbal form and make sure   │
│    the plan outline is high-level only and not a detailed breakdown.         │
│  • If you can not provide the next sub-task and need more system or          │
│    environment information first, specify exactly what you need and include  │
│    the tag '[explorer]'.                                                     │
│  • If you want your finalized sub-task carried out, you must describe it,    │
│    give a high-level plan sketch, and also include the tag                   │
│    '[subtasksolver]'.                                                        │
│  • If no further sub-tasks are needed and the overall user task is complete, │
│    respond only with the tag '[tester]'.                                     │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │

│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Subtasksolver agent of the Multi Agent System.          │
│  • Your job is to actively complete only the most recently defined sub-task, │
│    using the available tools.                                                │
│  • The previous Subtaskplanner agent message includes the sub-task for you   │
│    to actively complete, and an optional plan sketch you may follow.         │
│  • Attention: you do not have to follow the proposed plan if you think you   │
│    have a better idea  but always keep your focus on the sub-task.           │
│  • Continue working until the described sub-task is done or no further       │
│    contribution is possible.                                                 │
│  • Also, verify whether the chosen sub-task is reasonable. If it needs       │
│    refinement, provide your feedback and include the tag '[subtaskplanner]'. │
│  • When finished, or you get stuck, write a summary of what you did and      │
│    include the tag '[subtaskplanner]'.                                       │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                         Code Execution Capabilities                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your are equipped with different tools for executing code.                │
│  • Do only use the tools to execute code. Code inside a Markdown Code Block  │
│    will not be executed.                                                     │
│  • Use Shell tool for simple, single bash commands. Do not use it for        │
│    commands that open interactive terminal programs (e.g. git log or man).   │
│  • Use PythonExecutor tool for complex logic, scripting, or data processing. │
│  • Use Terminal tool to execute commands in a persistent terminal instance   │
│    and get back the exact terminal screen. This is especially useful for     │
│    interactive programs such as git log, man, or vim.                        │

│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your role is the "Approver" agent of the Multi Agent System.              │
│  • Your job is to determine if the agents have done enough work on the task. │
│  • Make your decision based on all information gathered and the actions      │
│    performed by previous agents.                                             │
│  • Be critical and selective, approve only what you are completely sure of.  │
│  • If you decide that the task was not solved successfully, briefly explain  │
│    why and include the tag '[refiner]'.                                      │
│  • If you decide that not enough testing was done to ensure the task is      │
│    fully and successfully completed, briefly explain why and add the tag     │
│    '[tester]'.                                                               │
│  • If you decide that sufficient work was done, just reply briefly with the  │
│    tag '[summarizer]'.                                                       │
│  • Make one of the three decisions and include its tag in your response.     │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭─────────────────── logging - summarizer - system_message ────────────────────╮
│                                                                              │
│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │
│    and command-line related tasks.                                           │
│  • You, all other agents and the user operate on the same system.            │
│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your role is the "Summarizer" agent of the Multi Agent System.            │
│  • Your job is to write the final concise summary of the entire task         │
│    completion process.                                                       │
│  • Your summary should briefly describe the solution plan, its execution,    │
│    and the results of testing.                                               │
│  • Highlight key findings or lessons learned, if any.                        │

│ static unsigned char convertToDoomKey(unsigned int key) { switch (key) {     │
│ case '\r':  // Return key key = KEY_ENTER; break; case 27:    // Escape key  │
│ key = KEY_ESCAPE; break; case 0x4B:  // Left arrow key = KEY_LEFTARROW;      │
│ break; case 0x4D:  // Right arrow key = KEY_RIGHTARROW; break; case 0x48:    │
│ // Up arrow key = KEY_UPARROW; break; case 0x50:  // Down arrow key =        │
│ KEY_DOWNARROW; break; case 0x1D:  // Ctrl key = KEY_FIRE; break; case ' ':   │
│ // Space key = KEY_USE; break; case 0x2A:  // Left Shift case 0x36:  //      │
│ Right Shift key = KEY_RSHIFT; break; default: key = (key); break; }          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  return key;                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ static void addKeyToQueue(int pressed, unsigned int keyCode) { unsigned char │
│ key = convertToDoomKey(keyCode);                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  unsigned short keyData = (pressed &lt;&lt; 8) | key;                        │
│                                                                              │
│  s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueWriteIndex] = keyData;                                 │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex++;                                                     │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ image_size;      // Headers + image data                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Allocate and initialize the buffer to 0                                  │
│  uint8_t* bmp = (uint8_t*)malloc(file_size+2);                               │
│  for (int i = 0; i &lt; 1024; i++) {                                         │
│    bmp[i] = 0;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│  if (!bmp) {                                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Memory allocation failed\n&quot;);                │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  bmp = bmp+2;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Use uint32_t array for headers (with proper byte offsets)                │
│  uint16_t* signature = (uint16_t*)bmp;                                       │
│  uint32_t* header = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 2);                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // File header (14 bytes)                                                   │
│  *signature = 0x4D42;                      // &#x27;BM&#x27; in              │
│  little-endian                                                               │
│  header[0] = file_size;                    // File size                      │
│  header[1] = 0;                            // Reserved                       │
│  header[2] = 54;                           // Offset to pixel data           │
│                                                                              │
│  // DIB header (40 bytes)                                                    │
│  header[3] = 40;                           // DIB header size                │
│  header[4] = width;                        // Width                          │
│  header[5] = -height;                      // Height (negative for           │
│  top-down)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  // Color planes (16-bit) and bits per pixel (16-bit)                        │
│  uint16_t* color_data = (uint16_t*)(bmp + 26);                               │
│  color_data[0] = 1;                        // Color planes                   │
│  color_data[1] = 32;                       // Bits per pixel (32-bit BGRA)   │
│                                                                              │
│  header[7] = 0;                            // No compression                 │
│  header[8] = image_size;                   // Image size                     │
│  header[9] = 0;                            // X pixels per meter             │
│  header[10] = 0;                           // Y pixels per meter             │
│  header[11] = 0;                           // Total colors                   │
│  header[12] = 0;                           // Important colors               │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write pixel data directly (convert RGBA to BGRA)                         │
│  uint32_t* pixel_data = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 54);                               │
│  for (int y = 0; y &lt; height; y++) {                                       │
│      for (int x = 0; x &lt; width; x++) {                                    │
│          uint32_t pixel = buffer[y * width + x];                             │
│          // Swap R and B (RGBA to BGRA)                                      │
│          *pixel_data++ = pixel;                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write to file                                                            │
│  FILE* file = fopen(filename, &quot;wb&quot;);                               │
│  if (!file) {                                                                │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Cannot open file %s\n&quot;, filename);           │
│      free(bmp);                                                              │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  size_t written = fwrite(bmp, 1, file_size, file);                           │
│  if (written != file_size) {                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Error writing file: %zu of %u bytes               │
│  written\n&quot;, written, file_size);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  fclose(file);                                                               │
│  free(bmp-2);                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ void DG_Init() { memset(s_KeyQueue, 0, KEYQUEUE_SIZE * sizeof(unsigned       │
│ short)); // No X11 initialization needed printf("DoomGeneric initialized.    │
│ Frames will be saved to /tmp/frame.bmp\n"); }                                │
│                                                                              │
│ int drawk = 0; void DG_DrawFrame() { // Save the current frame to a BMP file │
│ if (drawk++%10 == 0) { writeBMPFile("/tmp/frame.bmp",                        │
│ (uint32_t*)DG_ScreenBuffer, DOOMGENERIC_RESX, DOOMGENERIC_RESY); }           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Handle keyboard input if needed                                          │
│  // This would need to be replaced with a different input method             │
│  // since we&#x27;re not using X11 anymore                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ void DG_SleepMs(uint32_t ms) { // Spinloop implementation for sleep using    │
│ inline assembly // Assuming 3GHz processor (3 billion cycles per second) //  │
│ 1ms = 3 million cycles uint64_t cycles_per_ms = 3; uint64_t total_cycles =   │
│ cycles_per_ms * ms;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  volatile uint64_t i;                                                        │
│  for (i = 0; i &lt; total_cycles; i++)                                       │
│  {                                                                           │
│      // Use volatile inline assembly to ensure the loop isn&#x27;t           │
│  optimized away                                                              │
│      __asm__ volatile(&quot;&quot; ::: &quot;memory&quot;);                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ int ms_ctr = 0;; uint32_t DG_GetTicksMs() { return ms_ctr++; }               │
│                                                                              │
│ int DG_GetKey(int* pressed, unsigned char* doomKey) { if                     │
│ (s_KeyQueueReadIndex == s_KeyQueueWriteIndex) { //key queue is empty return  │
│ 0; } else { unsigned short keyData = s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueReadIndex];        │
│ s_KeyQueueReadIndex++; s_KeyQueueReadIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      *pressed = keyData &gt;&gt; 8;                                          │
│      *doomKey = keyData &amp; 0xFF;                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      return 1;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ <output> /**                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • MIPS Virtual Machine in JavaScript                                        │
│  • This VM implements the core MIPS instruction set for use in a             │
│    MIPS-to-JavaScript compiler.                                              │
│  • It handles register operations, memory access, and program flow control.  │
│    */                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ const fs = require('fs');                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ class MIPSVM { constructor() { // Initialize 32 general-purpose registers    │
│ (R0-R31) // R0 is always 0 in MIPS this.registers = new Array(32).fill(0);   │
│ this.registers[29] = 0x7FFFFFFC                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Register names for debugging                                             │
│  this.regNames = [                                                           │
│    &quot;zero&quot;, &quot;at&quot;, &quot;v0&quot;, &quot;v1&quot;,         │
│  &quot;a0&quot;, &quot;a1&quot;, &quot;a2&quot;, &quot;a3&quot;,             │
│    &quot;t0&quot;, &quot;t1&quot;, &quot;t2&quot;, &quot;t3&quot;,           │
│  &quot;t4&quot;, &quot;t5&quot;, &quot;t6&quot;, &quot;t7&quot;,             │
│    &quot;s0&quot;, &quot;s1&quot;, &quot;s2&quot;, &quot;s3&quot;,           │
│  &quot;s4&quot;, &quot;s5&quot;, &quot;s6&quot;, &quot;s7&quot;,             │
│    &quot;t8&quot;, &quot;t9&quot;, &quot;k0&quot;, &quot;k1&quot;,           │
│  &quot;gp&quot;, &quot;sp&quot;, &quot;fp&quot;, &quot;ra&quot;              │
│  ];                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  // Special registers                                                        │
│  this.hi = 0;  // High result register                                       │
│  this.lo = 0;  // Low result register                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  // Program counter                                                          │
│  this.pc = 0;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Memory (simplified as an object mapping addresses to values)             │
│  this.memory = {};                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Exception program counter                                                │
│  this.epc = 0;                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  // File descriptors for syscalls                                            │
│  this.fileDescriptors = {                                                    │
│    0: process.stdin.fd,   // Standard input                                  │
│    1: process.stdout.fd,  // Standard output                                 │
│    2: process.stderr.fd   // Standard error                                  │
│  };                                                                          │
│  this.fdToStreamMap = {                                                      │
│    [process.stdin.fd]: process.stdin,                                        │
│    [process.stdout.fd]: process.stdout,                                      │
│    [process.stderr.fd]: process.stderr                                       │
│  };                                                                          │
│  this.fileCache = {};                                                        │
│  this.fdCounter = 3;  // Start after stdin, stdout, stderr                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Helper methods                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ signExtend16(value) { // Sign-extend a 16-bit value to 32 bits value &=      │
│ 0xFFFF; // Ensure it's 16 bits return (value & 0x8000) ? (value |            │
│ 0xFFFF0000) : value; }                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ // Print the current state of the registers printRegisters() { for (let i =  │
│ 0; i < 32; i++) { console.log($${i} (${this.regNames[i]}) =                  │
│ 0x${this.registers[i].toString(16).padStart(8, &#x27;0&#x27;)}               │
│ (${this.registers[i]})); } console.log(HI =                                  │
│ 0x${this.hi.toString(16).padStart(8, &#x27;0&#x27;)} (${this.hi}));          │
│ console.log(LO = 0x${this.lo.toString(16).padStart(8, &#x27;0&#x27;)}        │
│ (${this.lo})); console.log(PC = 0x${this.pc.toString(16).padStart(8,         │
│ &#x27;0&#x27;)}); }                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- ALU Operations ----                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ add(rs, rt, rd) { // ADD rd,rs,rt - Add (with overflow) const result =       │
│ (this.registers[rs] + this.registers[rt]) | 0; // Force 32-bit signed math   │
│ this.registers[rd] = result; // Note: In a real MIPS CPU, this would check   │
│ for overflow }                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ addi(rt, rs, imm) { // ADDI rt,rs,imm - Add Immediate (with overflow) const  │
│ signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm); const result = (this.registers[rs] +    │
│ signExtImm) | 0; // Force 32-bit signed math this.registers[rt] = result; // │
│ Note: In a real MIPS CPU, this would check for overflow }                    │
│                                                                              │
│ addiu(rt, rs, imm) { // ADDIU rt,rs,imm - Add Immediate Unsigned (no         │
│ overflow) const signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm); this.registers[rt] =    │
│ (this.registers[rs] + signExtImm) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned math }     │
│                                                                              │
│ addu(rs, rt, rd) { // ADDU rd,rs,rt - Add Unsigned (no overflow)             │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] + this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; //     │
│ Force 32-bit unsigned math }                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ and(rs, rt, rd) { // AND rd,rs,rt - Bitwise AND this.registers[rd] =         │
│ (this.registers[rs] & this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; }                           │
│                                                                              │
│ andi(rt, rs, imm) { // ANDI rt,rs,imm - Bitwise AND Immediate // Note:       │
│ Zero-extended for this operation this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] &   │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF); }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ lui(rt, imm) { // LUI rt,imm - Load Upper Immediate this.registers[rt] =     │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF) << 16; }                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ nor(rs, rt, rd) { // NOR rd,rs,rt - Bitwise NOR this.registers[rd] =         │
│ ~(this.registers[rs] | this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned } │
│                                                                              │
│ or(rs, rt, rd) { // OR rd,rs,rt - Bitwise OR this.registers[rd] =            │
│ (this.registers[rs] | this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; }                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ori(rt, rs, imm) { // ORI rt,rs,imm - Bitwise OR Immediate // Note:          │
│ Zero-extended for this operation this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] |   │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF); }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ slt(rs, rt, rd) { // SLT rd,rs,rt - Set on Less Than (signed) // Convert to  │
│ signed values for comparison let valRs = this.registers[rs]; let valRt =     │
│ this.registers[rt];                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // JavaScript handles signed comparison natively                            │
│  this.registers[rd] = (valRs | 0) &lt; (valRt | 0) ? 1 : 0;                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ slti(rt, rs, imm) { // SLTI rt,rs,imm - Set on Less Than Immediate (signed)  │
│ const signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm); // JavaScript handles signed      │
│ comparison natively this.registers[rt] = (this.registers[rs] | 0) <          │
│ (signExtImm | 0) ? 1 : 0; }                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ sltiu(rt, rs, imm) { // SLTIU rt,rs,imm - Set on Less Than Immediate         │
│ Unsigned const signExtImm = this.signExtend16(imm) >>> 0; // Force unsigned  │
│ this.registers[rt] = (this.registers[rs] >>> 0) < signExtImm ? 1 : 0; }      │
│                                                                              │
│ sltu(rs, rt, rd) { // SLTU rd,rs,rt - Set on Less Than Unsigned              │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] >>> 0) < (this.registers[rt] >>> 0) │
│ ? 1 : 0; }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sub(rs, rt, rd) { // SUB rd,rs,rt - Subtract (with overflow)                 │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] - this.registers[rt]) | 0; // Force │
│ 32-bit signed // Note: In a real MIPS CPU, this would check for overflow }   │
│                                                                              │
│ subu(rs, rt, rd) { // SUBU rd,rs,rt - Subtract Unsigned (no overflow)        │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rs] - this.registers[rt]) >>> 0; //     │
│ Force 32-bit unsigned }                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ xor(rs, rt, rd) { // XOR rd,rs,rt - Bitwise XOR this.registers[rd] =         │
│ this.registers[rs] ^ this.registers[rt]; }                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ xori(rt, rs, imm) { // XORI rt,rs,imm - Bitwise XOR Immediate // Note:       │
│ Zero-extended for this operation this.registers[rt] = this.registers[rs] ^   │
│ (imm & 0xFFFF); }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Shifter Operations ----                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ sll(rt, rd, sa) { // SLL rd,rt,sa - Shift Left Logical this.registers[rd] =  │
│ (this.registers[rt] << sa) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned }                 │
│                                                                              │
│ sllv(rs, rt, rd) { // SLLV rd,rt,rs - Shift Left Logical Variable const      │
│ shift = this.registers[rs] & 0x1F; // Only bottom 5 bits used                │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] << shift) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit    │
│ unsigned }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sra(rt, rd, sa) { // SRA rd,rt,sa - Shift Right Arithmetic // JavaScript >>  │
│ is arithmetic shift right this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >> sa) |  │
│ 0; // Force 32-bit signed }                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ srav(rs, rt, rd) { // SRAV rd,rt,rs - Shift Right Arithmetic Variable const  │
│ shift = this.registers[rs] & 0x1F; // Only bottom 5 bits used                │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >> shift) | 0; // Force 32-bit      │
│ signed }                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ srl(rt, rd, sa) { // SRL rd,rt,sa - Shift Right Logical // JavaScript >>> is │
│ logical shift right this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >>> sa) >>> 0;  │
│ // Force 32-bit unsigned }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ srlv(rs, rt, rd) { // SRLV rd,rt,rs - Shift Right Logical Variable const     │
│ shift = this.registers[rs] & 0x1F; // Only bottom 5 bits used                │
│ this.registers[rd] = (this.registers[rt] >>> shift) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit   │
│ unsigned }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Multiply Operations ----                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ div(rs, rt) { // DIV rs,rt - Divide (signed) if (this.registers[rt] === 0) { │
│ // Division by zero - implementation defined this.lo = 0; this.hi = 0;       │
│ return; }                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // JavaScript handles signed division natively                              │
│  this.lo = ((this.registers[rs] | 0) / (this.registers[rt] | 0)) | 0; //     │
│  Quotient                                                                    │
│  this.hi = ((this.registers[rs] | 0) % (this.registers[rt] | 0)) | 0; //     │
│  Remainder                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ divu(rs, rt) { // DIVU rs,rt - Divide Unsigned if (this.registers[rt] === 0) │
│ { // Division by zero - implementation defined this.lo = 0; this.hi = 0;     │
│ return; }                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  const unsignedRs = this.registers[rs] &gt;&gt;&gt; 0;                       │
│  const unsignedRt = this.registers[rt] &gt;&gt;&gt; 0;                       │
│                                                                              │
│  this.lo = Math.floor(unsignedRs / unsignedRt) &gt;&gt;&gt; 0; // Quotient   │
│  this.hi = (unsignedRs % unsignedRt) &gt;&gt;&gt; 0; // Remainder            │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ mfhi(rd) { // MFHI rd - Move From HI this.registers[rd] = this.hi; }         │
│                                                                              │
│ mflo(rd) { // MFLO rd - Move From LO this.registers[rd] = this.lo; }         │
│                                                                              │
│ ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── │
│ mthi(rs) { // MTHI rs - Move To HI this.hi = this.registers[rs]; }           │
│                                                                              │
│ mtlo(rs) { // MTLO rs - Move To LO this.lo = this.registers[rs]; }           │
│                                                                              │
│ mult(rs, rt) { // MULT rs,rt - Multiply (signed) // JavaScript handles       │
│ signed multiplication natively const result = BigInt(this.registers[rs] | 0) │
│ * BigInt(this.registers[rt] | 0);                                            │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Store 64-bit result in HI/LO                                             │
│  this.lo = Number(result &amp; BigInt(0xFFFFFFFF));                          │
│  this.hi = Number((result &gt;&gt; BigInt(32)) &amp; BigInt(0xFFFFFFFF));    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ multu(rs, rt) { // MULTU rs,rt - Multiply Unsigned const result =            │
│ BigInt(this.registers[rs] >>> 0) * BigInt(this.registers[rt] >>> 0);         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Store 64-bit result in HI/LO                                             │
│  this.lo = Number(result &amp; BigInt(0xFFFFFFFF));                          │
│  this.hi = Number((result &gt;&gt; BigInt(32)) &amp; BigInt(0xFFFFFFFF));    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Branch Operations ----                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ beq(rs, rt, offset) { // BEQ rs,rt,offset - Branch on Equal if               │
│ (this.registers[rs] === this.registers[rt]) { this.pc = (this.pc + (offset   │
│ << 2)) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned } }                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ beqz(rs, offset) { // BEQZ rs,offset - Branch if Equal to Zero if            │
│ (this.registers[rs] === 0) { this.pc = (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; //   │
│ Force 32-bit unsigned } }                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ bgez(rs, offset) { // BGEZ rs,offset - Branch on Greater Than or Equal to    │
│ Zero // Convert to signed for comparison if ((this.registers[rs] | 0) >= 0)  │
│ { this.pc = (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; } }                             │
│                                                                              │
│ bgezal(rs, offset) { // BGEZAL rs,offset - Branch on Greater Than or Equal   │
│ to Zero And Link // Convert to signed for comparison if ((this.registers[rs] │
│ | 0) >= 0) { this.registers[31] = this.pc; // Store return address in $ra    │
│ this.pc = (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; } }                               │
│                                                                              │
│ bgtz(rs, offset) { // BGTZ rs,offset - Branch on Greater Than Zero //        │
│ Convert to signed for comparison if ((this.registers[rs] | 0) > 0) { this.pc │
│ = (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; } }                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ blez(rs, offset) { // BLEZ rs,offset - Branch on Less Than or Equal to Zero  │
│ // Convert to signed for comparison if ((this.registers[rs] | 0) <= 0) {     │
│ this.pc = (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; } }                               │
│                                                                              │
│ bltz(rs, offset) { // BLTZ rs,offset - Branch on Less Than Zero // Convert   │
│ to signed for comparison if ((this.registers[rs] | 0) < 0) { this.pc =       │
│ (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; } }                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ bltzal(rs, offset) { // BLTZAL rs,offset - Branch on Less Than Zero And Link │
│ // Convert to signed for comparison if ((this.registers[rs] | 0) < 0) {      │
│ this.registers[31] = this.pc; // Store return address in $ra this.pc =       │
│ (this.pc + (offset << 2)) >>> 0; } }                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ bne(rs, rt, offset) { // BNE rs,rt,offset - Branch on Not Equal if           │
│ (this.registers[rs] !== this.registers[rt]) { this.pc = (this.pc + (offset   │
│ << 2)) >>> 0; } }                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Jump Operations ----                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ j(target) { // J target - Jump // Combine upper 4 bits of PC with 26-bit     │
│ target shifted left by 2 this.pc = ((this.pc & 0xF0000000) | (target << 2))  │
│ >>> 0; }                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ jal(target) { // JAL target - Jump and Link this.registers[31] = this.pc +   │
│ 4; // Store return address in $ra // Combine upper 4 bits of PC with 26-bit  │
│ target shifted left by 2 this.pc = ((this.pc & 0xF0000000) | (target << 2))  │
│ >>> 0; }                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ jalr(rs, rd = 31) { // JALR rs,rd - Jump and Link Register const temp =      │
│ this.registers[rs]; // Store jump target this.registers[rd] = this.pc + 4;   │
│ // Store return address this.pc = temp; }                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ jr(rs) { // JR rs - Jump Register this.pc = this.registers[rs]; }            │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Other Control Operations ----                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ movz(rs, rt, rd) { // MOVZ rd,rs,rt - Move if Zero if (this.registers[rt]    │
│ === 0) { this.registers[rd] = this.registers[rs]; } }                        │
│                                                                              │
│ movn(rs, rt, rd) { // MOVN rd,rs,rt - Move if Not Zero if                    │
│ (this.registers[rt] !== 0) { this.registers[rd] = this.registers[rs]; } }    │
│                                                                              │
│ teq(rs, rt, code = 0) { // TEQ rs,rt,code - Trap if Equal if                 │
│ (this.registers[rs] === this.registers[rt]) { this.epc = this.pc; // In a    │
│ real implementation, this would trigger a trap exception throw new Error(TEQ │
│ trap exception: registers ${this.regNames[rs]} and ${this.regNames[rt]} are  │
│ equal (code ${code})); } }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ break(code) { // BREAK - Breakpoint this.epc = this.pc; // In a real         │
│ implementation, this would trigger a breakpoint exception throw new          │
│ Error(BREAK exception (code ${code})); }                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ syscall(code) { // SYSCALL - System Call return this.handleSyscall(); }      │
│                                                                              │
│ // ---- Memory Access Operations ----                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ lb(rt, offset, base) { // LB rt,offset(base) - Load Byte (signed) const addr │
│ = (this.registers[base] + offset) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned const      │
│ wordAddr = addr & ~0x3; // Align to word boundary const byteOffset = addr &  │
│ 0x3; // Byte position within word (0-3)                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Get the word from memory                                                 │
│  const word = this.memory[wordAddr] || 0;                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // Extract byte using little-endian byte order                              │
│  let byteValue = (word &gt;&gt; (byteOffset * 8)) &amp; 0xFF;                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Sign-extend the byte to 32 bits                                          │
│  if (byteValue &amp; 0x80) {                                                 │
│    byteValue |= 0xFFFFFF00;                                                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  this.registers[rt] = byteValue;                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ lbu(rt, offset, base) { // LBU rt,offset(base) - Load Byte Unsigned const    │
│ addr = (this.registers[base] + offset) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned const │
│ wordAddr = addr & ~0x3; // Align to word boundary const byteOffset = addr &  │
│ 0x3; // Byte position within word (0-3)                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Get the word from memory                                                 │
│  const word = this.memory[wordAddr] || 0;                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // Extract byte using little-endian byte order and zero-extend              │
│  this.registers[rt] = (word &gt;&gt; (byteOffset * 8)) &amp; 0xFF;           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ lh(rt, offset, base) { // LH rt,offset(base) - Load Halfword (signed) const  │
│ addr = (this.registers[base] + offset) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  if (addr &amp; 0x1) {                                                       │
│    throw new Error(`Unaligned halfword access at 0x${addr.toString(16)}`);   │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  const wordAddr = addr &amp; ~0x3; // Align to word boundary                 │
│  const halfwordOffset = (addr &amp; 0x2) &gt;&gt; 1; // 0 for first          │
│  halfword, 1 for second                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  // Get the word from memory                                                 │
│  const word = this.memory[wordAddr] || 0;                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // Extract halfword using little-endian byte order                          │
│  let halfwordValue = (word &gt;&gt; (halfwordOffset * 16)) &amp; 0xFFFF;     │
│                                                                              │
│  // Sign-extend the halfword to 32 bits                                      │
│  if (halfwordValue &amp; 0x8000) {                                           │
│    halfwordValue |= 0xFFFF0000;                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  this.registers[rt] = halfwordValue;                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ lhu(rt, offset, base) { // LHU rt,offset(base) - Load Halfword Unsigned      │
│ const addr = (this.registers[base] + offset) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  if (addr &amp; 0x1) {                                                       │
│    throw new Error(`Unaligned halfword access at 0x${addr.toString(16)}`);   │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  const wordAddr = addr &amp; ~0x3; // Align to word boundary                 │
│  const halfwordOffset = (addr &amp; 0x2) &gt;&gt; 1; // 0 for first          │
│  halfword, 1 for second                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  // Get the word from memory                                                 │
│  const word = this.memory[wordAddr] || 0;                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // Extract halfword using little-endian byte order and zero-extend          │
│  this.registers[rt] = (word &gt;&gt; (halfwordOffset * 16)) &amp; 0xFFFF;    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ lw(rt, offset, base) { // LW rt,offset(base) - Load Word const addr =        │
│ (this.registers[base] + offset) >>> 0; // Force 32-bit unsigned              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  if (addr &amp; 0x3) {                                                       │
│    throw new Error(`Unaligned word access at 0x${addr.toString(16)}`);       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Load whole word directly                                                 │
│  this.registers[rt] = this.memory[addr] || 0;                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } lwl(rt, offset, base) { // LWL rt,offset(base) - Load Word Left // Load    │
│ the left (high-order) bytes of a register from memory                        │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      // Calculate effective address (ensure it's a 32-bit unsigned int)      │
│                                                                              │
│ /**                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • MIPS Virtual Machine in JavaScript                                        │
│  • This VM implements the core MIPS instruction set for use in a             │
│    MIPS-to-JavaScript compiler.                                              │
│  • It handles register operations, memory access, and program flow control.  │
│    */                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ const fs = require('fs');                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ class MIPSVM { constructor() { // Initialize 32 general-purpose registers    │
│ (R0-R31) // R0 is always 0 in MIPS this.registers = new Array(32).fill(0);   │
│ this.registers[29] = 0x7FFFFFFC                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Register names for debugging                                             │
│  this.regNames = [                                                           │
│    &quot;zero&quot;, &quot;at&quot;, &quot;v0&quot;, &quot;v1&quot;,         │

│  &quot;gp&quot;, &quot;sp&quot;, &quot;fp&quot;, &quot;ra&quot;              │
│  ];                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  // Special registers                                                        │
│  this.hi = 0;  // High result register                                       │
│  this.lo = 0;  // Low result register                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  // Program counter                                                          │
│  this.pc = 0;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Memory (simplified as an object mapping addresses to values)             │
│  this.memory = {};                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Exception program counter                                                │
│  this.epc = 0;                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  // File descriptors for syscalls                                            │
│  this.fileDescriptors = {                                                    │
│    0: process.stdin.fd,   // Standard input                                  │
│    1: process.stdout.fd,  // Standard output                                 │
│    2: process.stderr.fd   // Standard error                                  │
│  };                                                                          │
│  this.fdToStreamMap = {                                                      │
│    [process.stdin.fd]: process.stdin,                                        │
│    [process.stdout.fd]: process.stdout,                                      │
│    [process.stderr.fd]: process.stderr                                       │
│  };                                                                          │
│  this.fileCache = {};                                                        │
│  this.fdCounter = 3;  // Start after stdin, stdout, stderr                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│     • sudo apt-get update                                                    │
│     • sudo apt-get install -y gcc-mips-linux-gnu g++-mips-linux-gnu          │
│     • For 64-bit MIPS: gcc-mips64-linux-gnu g++-mips64-linux-gnu             │
│  • If you’re on a different distro, install equivalent packages (look for a  │

│  • The exact MIPS target (mipsel vs mips) and endianness should be confirmed │
│    to avoid ABI incompatibilities.                                           │
│  • If a cross-compiler is not available, plan to either install one or use a │
│    container image that includes a MIPS toolchain.                           │
│  • Patch minimal: adding doomgeneric_img.o to the SRC list is sufficient to  │
│    wire frame capture; ensure any needed headers or linker flags are         │
│    satisfied by the cross-toolchain.                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ What I need from you (to finalize the plan):                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • Confirm the target MIPS ABI (default to mipsel-linux-gnu for              │

│    cross-compiler cannot be installed.                                       │

│ you can apply to actually produce a doomgeneric_mips ELF from the existing   │

│ \n+#\n+# Cross-build target for MIPS                                         │
│ (doomgeneric_mips)\n+#\n+doomgeneric_mips:                                   │
│ OUTPUT=doomgeneric_mips\n+doomgeneric_mips:                                  │
│ CROSS_COMPILE=mipsel-linux-gnu-\n+doomgeneric_mips: all\n+\n+\n\nNotes:\n-   │
│ This approach uses a conventional CROSS_COMPILE prefix. If you prefer a      │
│ different cross-compiler (e.g., a different MIPS arch such as                │
│ mips64el-linux-gnu-), adjust CROSS_COMPILE accordingly before running make   │
│ doomgeneric_mips.\n- The host build will still work as before with the       │
│ default host compiler, since CROSS_COMPILE remains empty in that case, so CC │
│ resolves to the host compiler.\n\nWhat I did not do (yet)\n- I did not       │
│ install a MIPS cross-compiler in this environment (no privileged package     │
│ install or container tooling here). If you want me to execute the actual     │
│ build steps, I’ll need you to provide a toolchain or allow me to guide       │
│ and/or script the installation in your environment.\n- I did not patch for   │
│ removing X11 dependencies for cross builds in a more robust way. If you want │
│ a headless MIPS binary, we can further tailor the Makefile to:\n  - Exclude  │
│ doomgeneric_xlib.o in cross builds\n  - Remove -lX11 and any X11-specific    │
│ libraries from LIBS for the cross path\n  - Or switch to a DoomGeneric cross │
│ build variant that uses only the BMP frame writer                            │
│ (doomgeneric_img.c)\n\nValidation plan (once cross-compiler is available)\n- │
│ Prereqs:\n  - MIPS cross-compiler installed (e.g., sudo apt-get install -y   │
│ gcc-mipsel-linux-gnu g++-mipsel-linux-gnu)\n  - Sufficient sysroot or        │
│ libraries to satisfy the cross-compiler (libc, crt*.o, etc). For a simple    │
│ Doom run, you typically want a static or properly linked binary; depending   │
│ on your toolchain, you may need to tweak LDFLAGS or LIBS for cross.\n-       │

│ - node vm.js\n  - Observe stdout for program output\n  - Check               │
│ /tmp/frame.bmp after a few frames are drawn\n\nCaveats and notes\n- ABI and  │
│ endianness: I default to mipsel-linux-gnu (little-endian MIPS32) as a common │
│ choice for a cross path. If you need big-endian or a different MIPS variant  │
│ (e.g., MIPS64), adjust CROSS_COMPILE accordingly (e.g., mips64el-linux-gnu-  │
│ or mips-linux-gnu- for non-el).\n- Libraries: If doomgeneric_xlib.o is       │
│ included for host builds, you may need X11 libraries to link. For a cross    │
│ MIPS build run with only the BMP writer, you’ll likely want to ensure        │
│ doomgeneric_xlib.o is not included or X11 libs are not linked. The patch     │

│  • Clear notes on headless builds and container options.                     │
│                                                                              │
│ Deliverables                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  1 Dockerfile (ready to apply) Code (paste into a file named Dockerfile in   │
│    your repo root or in a dedicated container-dir):                          │
│                                                                              │
│ FROM ubuntu:22.04                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                Essential tools and a MIPS cross-compiler                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ RUN apt-get update &&                                                        │
│ DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive                                               │
│ apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends                                   │
│ build-essential                                                              │
│ gcc-mipsel-linux-gnu                                                         │

│ patch                                                                        │

│ unzip                                                                        │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  Working directory for the cross-build                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃           Copy sources (adjust if you keep sources elsewhere)            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   If you keep the DoomGeneric sources in /app/doomgeneric on the host,   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃          you can build with a bind mount instead of COPY here.           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ COPY . /workspace                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Optional: patch Makefile to ensure doomgeneric_img.o is built and to add ┃ │
│ ┃                             a cross target.                              ┃ │
