│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   You can apply patches via a patch file, or rely on the patch script    ┃ │
│ ┃                             described below.                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   If you have a patch file patch.cross.diff, uncomment the next line:    ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃            COPY patch.cross.diff /workspace/patch.cross.diff             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                     RUN patch -p0 < patch.cross.diff                     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 Cross-build helper environment variable                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ENV CROSS_COMPILE=mipsel-linux-gnu-                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  Build target (cross-compile). The Makefile must support CC/CXX/LDFLAGS  ┃ │
│ ┃                                overrides.                                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ RUN make -C doomgeneric/doomgeneric                                          │
│ doomgeneric_mips                                                             │
│ CC=${CROSS_COMPILE}gcc                                                       │
│ CXX=${CROSS_COMPILE}g++                                                      │
│ AR=${CROSS_COMPILE}ar                                                        │
│ LD=${CROSS_COMPILE}ld                                                        │
│ STRIP=${CROSS_COMPILE}strip                                                  │
│ -j$(nproc)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    Expose a predictable location for the produced binary (inside the     ┃ │
│ ┃                                container)                                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                Host extraction will copy this out later.                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ RUN mkdir -p /workspace/dist RUN cp -v                                       │
│ /workspace/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/doomgeneric_mips /workspace/dist/         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    By default, shells into the container for manual inspect if needed    ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ CMD ["/bin/bash"]                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes:                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • The Makefile in doomgeneric/doomgeneric must support cross-compilation    │
│    variables (CC, CXX, AR, LD, etc.) to correctly produce a MIPS ELF. If     │
│    your current Makefile doesn't, you’ll need to apply a patch (see patch    │
│    plan below) to introduce a cross-build target (doomgeneric_mips) and      │
│    ensure doomgeneric_img.o is compiled and linked.                          │
│  • doomgeneric_img.c is assumed to be part of the DoomGeneric sources. If it │
│    isn’t automatically included in the cross-target, patch the Makefile as   │
│    described in the patch plan.                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  2 Patch plan (Makefile changes) to wire doomgeneric_img and add a cross     │
│    target Goal: Ensure doomgeneric_img.c is compiled into the final binary   │
│    and add a cross build target doomgeneric_mips that uses the MIPS          │
│    cross-compiler.                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Patch concept (unified diff style; adapt to your actual Makefile structure): │
│                                                                              │
│  • File: doomgeneric/doomgeneric/Makefile                                    │
│  • Change: Ensure doomgeneric_img.o is part of the object list ---           │
│    a/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/Makefile +++ b/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/Makefile │
│    @@                                                                        │
│  • OBJS = doomgeneric.o doomgeneric_xlib.o doomgeneric_main.o                │
│    doomgeneric_other.o ...                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • OBJS = doomgeneric.o doomgeneric_xlib.o doomgeneric_main.o                │
│    doomgeneric_other.o doomgeneric_img.o ...                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • Change: Add a cross-compile target doomgeneric_mips and honor a           │
│    cross-compiler prefix if provided --- a/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/Makefile  │
│    +++ b/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/Makefile @@                                 │
│  • all: $(OBJS)                                                              │
│  •                                                                           │
│     $(CC) -o doomgeneric $(OBJS) $(LDFLAGS)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • all: $(OBJS)                                                              │
│  • ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│    ┃            Cross-build target for MIPS (doomgeneric_mips)             ┃ │
│    ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│  • doomgeneric_mips: CROSS_COMPILE ?= mipsel-linux-gnu-                      │
│  • doomgeneric_mips: $(OBJS)                                                 │
│  •                                                                           │
│     $(CROSS_COMPILE)gcc -o doomgeneric_mips $(OBJS) $(LDFLAGS)               │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • doomgeneric: $(OBJS)                                                      │
│  •                                                                           │
│     $(CC) -o $@ $(OBJS)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes:                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • The exact variable names may differ in your Makefile. The key is to       │
│    ensure:                                                                   │
│     • doomgeneric_img.o is included in OBJS (so the frame-capture hook is    │
│       compiled and linked).                                                  │
│     • A cross-target doomgeneric_mips exists and uses the cross-compiler     │
│       prefix (CROSS_COMPILE or individual CC/CXX/LD variables) to produce a  │
│       MIPS ELF.                                                              │
│     • The output binary is named doomgeneric_mips (as vm.js expects) and is  │
│       placed in the same directory as other binaries or in a predictable     │
│       path (e.g., doomgeneric/doomgeneric/doomgeneric_mips).                 │
│  • If your Makefile doesn’t easily support a cross target, you can add a     │
│    separate cross-build Makefile fragment or a small wrapper script          │
│    (build_cross.sh) that invokes the same compiler with explicit flags.      │
│                                                                              │
│  3 Minimal host-friendly build-and-run script (example) This script is       │
│    optional if you prefer manual Docker/Makefile steps, but it makes the     │
│    workflow repeatable.                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Script: build_and_run_mips.sh                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/usr/bin/env bash set -euo pipefail                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ROOT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"                     │
│ IMAGE_NAME="doomgeneric-mips-builder" DIST_DIR="${ROOT_DIR}/dist"            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃             Build with Docker (container-based cross-build)              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ cat <<EOF > Dockerfile.cross FROM ubuntu:22.04                               │
│                                                                              │
│ RUN apt-get update &&                                                        │

│                                                                              │
│  • Build (container-based cross-build):                                      │
│     • Save the Dockerfile (as above) or reuse your existing one.             │
│     • Build the image: docker build -t doomgeneric-mips-builder .            │

│     • Ensure doomgeneric_mips is in the expected location for vm.js (the     │
│       host path vm.js expects). If vm.js looks in the same dir as its own    │
│       execution, you can copy it next to vm.js: cp dist/doomgeneric_mips .   │
│     • Run: node vm.js                                                        │

│     • stdout from the MIPS DoomGeneric binary will be printed to the         │
│       terminal (as VM stdout is captured by vm.js).                          │
│     • Frames will be written to /tmp/frame.bmp by the doomgeneric_img.c hook │
│       during rendering, exactly as implemented in DoomGeneric.               │
│                                                                              │
│  4 Verification, caveats, and notes                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • ABI and target                                                            │
│     • Default target: MIPS little-endian (mipsel). If you prefer a different │
│       MIPS variant, please specify (e.g., mips-64el or big-endian variants)  │
│       and I’ll adjust the toolchain and Makefile patches accordingly.        │
│  • Toolchain availability                                                    │
│     • The container approach relies on a MIPS cross-compiler inside the      │
│       Docker image. If you cannot use Docker, I can provide a host-based     │
│       cross-compile patch (with a detailed, alternate set of commands)       │
│       instead.                                                               │
│  • DoomGeneric Makefile compatibility                                        │
│     • The success of this plan hinges on the DoomGeneric Makefile accepting  │
│       overrides for CC/CXX/LD and on having a cross-target doomgeneric_mips. │
│       If your current Makefile structure differs, I’ll tailor the patch      │
│       accordingly. The patch plan above is intentionally generic to be       │
│       adaptable.                                                             │
│  • Frame capture                                                             │
│     • doomgeneric_img.c writes frames to /tmp/frame.bmp via the BMP writer.  │
│       Ensure the container and the host user have permission to write to     │
│       /tmp/frame.bmp during execution. If running inside Docker, /tmp on the │
│       container is isolated from the host; you’ll want to bind-mount /tmp    │
│       from host or adjust the path to a shared directory. The host-run       │
│       scenario assumes /tmp/frame.bmp on the host; if you want frames in the │
│       container, adjust doomgeneric_img.c and/or mount a shared path (e.g.,  │
│       -v /tmp:/tmp in docker run).                                           │
│  • Data availability                                                         │
│     • vm.js will load doomgeneric_mips and access /app/doom.wad as the Doom  │
│       data source. Ensure the container’s filesystem view includes           │
│       /app/doom.wad or adjust the VM/workspace paths accordingly.            │
│  • Documentation and next steps                                              │
│     • If you’d like, I can provide:                                          │

│          I can see its current contents).                                    │

│          alternative if you prefer).                                         │

│ known path.\n  - The container patches and builds DoomGeneric using a        │
│ cross-compiler.\n  - The final doomgeneric_mips binary is copied out to a    │
│ host-writable location for vm.js.\n\nDockerfile (save as                     │
│ Dockerfile)\n--------------------------------\nFROM ubuntu:22.04\n\n# Avoid  │
│ interactive prompts\nENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive\n\n# Install         │

│ -y --no-install-recommends \\n      build-essential \\n                      │
│ gcc-mipsel-linux-gnu \\n      g++-mipsel-linux-gnu \\n      make \\n         │

│ /var/lib/apt/lists/\n\n# Work in a dedicated workspace\nWORKDIR              │
│ /workspace\n\n# Helpers: patcher and build script (will be provided          │

│ build_mips.sh /workspace/build_mips.sh\nRUN chmod +x                         │
│ /workspace/patch_makefile.sh /workspace/build_mips.sh\n\n# By default, the   │
│ container will start a shell.\nCMD                                           │
│ ["/bin/bash"]\n--------------------------------\n\n2) Patch plan and patch   │
│ template (Makefile changes)\n- Goal: Ensure the MIPS cross-compiler is used, │
│ doomgeneric_img.c is compiled into the binary, and a new target              │
│ doomgeneric_mips is provided for cross-building.\n\nPatch plan (instructions │
│ you can apply with git/diff)\n- Objective 1: Wire doomgeneric_img.c into the │
│ build\n  - Add doomgeneric_img.o to the OBJS list (or to the equivalent      │
│ object list in the DoomGeneric Makefile that lists all .o files to be linked │
│ into the final executable).\n  - Ensure doomgeneric_img.c is in the same     │
│ compilation suite (i.e., the build system is calling the compiler for        │
│ doomgeneric_img.c).\n\n- Objective 2: Add a cross-build target\n  -          │
│ Introduce CROSS_COMPILE support (default to mipsel-linux-gnu-).\n  - Define  │
│ a doomgeneric_mips target that links all OBJS with the cross-compiler to     │
│ emit an ELF named doomgeneric_mips.\n  - Ensure the output path is           │
│ predictable (e.g., the final binary ends up in the repository’s              │
│ doomgeneric/doomgeneric_mips or a dedicated build dir).\n\n- Objective 3:    │
│ Ensure the cross compile uses proper flags\n  - CC := $(CROSS_COMPILE)gcc\n  │
│ - CXX := $(CROSS_COMPILE)g++\n  - AR := $(CROSS_COMPILE)ar\n  - LD :=        │
│ $(CROSS_COMPILE)ld\n  - STRIP := $(CROSS_COMPILE)strip\n  - Optional:        │
│ -mips32r2 -EL or -mabi=32 depending on the target (default to little-endian  │
│ mipsel; you can adjust if needed).\n\n- Patch example (diff-like, to adapt   │
│ to your actual Makefile)\nNote: This is a template; you should adapt it to   │
│ your real Makefile structure.\n\n--- Begin Patch Template ---\n** Begin      │
│ Patch\n*** Update File: Makefile\n@@\n- OBJS = doomgeneric.o                 │
│ doomgeneric_xlib.o ... other.o\n+ OBJS = doomgeneric.o doomgeneric_xlib.o    │
│ doomgeneric_img.o ... other.o\n \n+ # Cross-compile support (default to      │
│ mipsel)\n+ CROSS_COMPILE ?= mipsel-linux-gnu-\n+ CC :=                       │
│ $(CROSS_COMPILE)gcc\n+ CXX := $(CROSS_COMPILE)g++\n+ AR :=                   │
│ $(CROSS_COMPILE)ar\n+ LD := $(CROSS_COMPILE)ld\n+ STRIP :=                   │
│ $(CROSS_COMPILE)strip\n+\n+ # New cross-build target\n+ doomgeneric_mips:    │
│ $(OBJS)\n+     $(CC) -o $@ $(OBJS) $(LDFLAGS)\n*** End Patch Template        │
│ ---\n--------------------------------\n\n- Patch usage notes:\n  - Save      │
│ patch_makefile.sh to apply patches (see script below) or manually edit       │
│ Makefile with the changes above.\n  - Ensure any existing rules accommodate  │
│ the new target and that your build system respects CROSS_COMPILE when        │

│ host-writable location.\n\nbuild_mips.sh (save as                            │
│ build_mips.sh)\n--------------------------------\n#!/bin/bash\nset -euo      │
│ pipefail\n\n# Directory wiring\n# The host will mount the DoomGeneric source │
│ at                                                                           │
│ $SOURCE_ROOT\nSOURCE_ROOT="${SOURCE_ROOT:-/workspace/doomgeneric_source}"\nB │
│ UILD_ROOT="${BUILD_ROOT:-/workspace/build}"\nOUTPUT_ROOT="${OUTPUT_ROOT:-/wo │
│ rkspace/doomgeneric_mips_output}"\n\n# Ensure output directory exists\nmkdir │
│ -p "$OUTPUT_ROOT"\n\n# Enter the DoomGeneric build directory (adjust if your │
│ tree layout differs)\ncd "$SOURCE_ROOT/doomgeneric/doomgeneric" || {\n  echo │
│ "Failed to enter DoomGeneric build dir. Is SOURCE_ROOT correct?"\n  exit     │
│ 1\n}\n\n# Patch the Makefile to ensure doomgeneric_img.o is included and add │
│ cross target\necho "Patching Makefile to wire doomgeneric_img.o and cross    │
│ target..."\n/workspace/patch_makefile.sh\n\n# Clean and build for            │
│ MIPS\nexport CROSS_COMPILE="${CROSS_COMPILE:-mipsel-linux-gnu-}"\necho       │
│ "Using CROSS_COMPILE=$CROSS_COMPILE"\n\nmake clean\n# Build the cross target │
│ (doomgeneric_mips)\nmake doomgeneric_mips CC="${CROSS_COMPILE}gcc"           │
│ CXX="${CROSS_COMPILE}g++" AR="${CROSS_COMPILE}ar"                            │
│ RANLIB="${CROSS_COMPILE}ranlib" -j$(nproc)\n\n# Copy the resulting binary to │
│ a host-accessible location\nif [ -f "doomgeneric_mips" ]; then\n  cp         │
│ doomgeneric_mips "$OUTPUT_ROOT/doomgeneric_mips"\n  echo "Exported           │
│ /workspace/doomgeneric_mips_output/doomgeneric_mips"\nelse\n  echo "Error:   │
│ doomgeneric_mips binary not found after build."\n  ls -la\n  exit            │
│ 1\nfi\n--------------------------------\n\n4) Patch script                   │
│ (patch_makefile.sh)\n- Purpose: provide a lightweight patch application to   │
│ Makefile (idempotent if run multiple times).\n\npatch_makefile.sh (save as   │
│ patch_makefile.sh)\n--------------------------------\n#!/bin/bash\nset -euo  │
│ pipefail\nMAKEFILE="Makefile"\n\n# Simple guard: only patch if a marker is   │
│ not present\nif grep -q "DOOMGENERIC_MIPS_PATCHED" "$MAKEFILE"; then\n  echo │
│ "Makefile already patched."\n  exit 0\nfi\n\ncat > patch.diff                │

│                                                                              │
│  • Objective 1: Wire doomgeneric_img.c into the build                        │

│     • Define a doomgeneric_mips target that links all OBJS with the          │
│       cross-compiler to emit an ELF named doomgeneric_mips.                  │

│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃        The host will mount the DoomGeneric source at $SOURCE_ROOT        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ SOURCE_ROOT="${SOURCE_ROOT:-/workspace/doomgeneric_source}"                  │
│ BUILD_ROOT="${BUILD_ROOT:-/workspace/build}"                                 │
│ OUTPUT_ROOT="${OUTPUT_ROOT:-/workspace/doomgeneric_mips_output}"             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      Ensure output directory exists                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_ROOT"                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    Enter the DoomGeneric build directory (adjust if your tree layout     ┃ │
│ ┃                                 differs)                                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ cd "$SOURCE_ROOT/doomgeneric/doomgeneric" || { echo "Failed to enter         │
│ DoomGeneric build dir. Is SOURCE_ROOT correct?" exit 1 }                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Patch the Makefile to ensure doomgeneric_img.o is included and add cross ┃ │
│ ┃                                  target                                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ echo "Patching Makefile to wire doomgeneric_img.o and cross target..."       │
│ /workspace/patch_makefile.sh                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                         Clean and build for MIPS                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ export CROSS_COMPILE="${CROSS_COMPILE:-mipsel-linux-gnu-}" echo "Using       │
│ CROSS_COMPILE=$CROSS_COMPILE"                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ make clean                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                Build the cross target (doomgeneric_mips)                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ make doomgeneric_mips CC="${CROSS_COMPILE}gcc" CXX="${CROSS_COMPILE}g++"     │
│ AR="${CROSS_COMPILE}ar" RANLIB="${CROSS_COMPILE}ranlib" -j$(nproc)           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃         Copy the resulting binary to a host-accessible location          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│            if [ -f "doomgeneric_mips" ]; then cp doomgeneric_mips            │
│                "$OUTPUT_ROOT/doomgeneric_mips" echo "Exported                │
│    /workspace/doomgeneric_mips_output/doomgeneric_mips" else echo "Error:    │
│       doomgeneric_mips binary not found after build." ls -la exit 1 fi       │
│                                                                              │
│  4 Patch script (patch_makefile.sh)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Purpose: provide a lightweight patch application to Makefile (idempotent  │
│    if run multiple times).                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                patch_makefile.sh (save as patch_makefile.sh)                 │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/bin/bash set -euo pipefail MAKEFILE="Makefile"                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃           Simple guard: only patch if a marker is not present            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if grep -q "DOOMGENERIC_MIPS_PATCHED" "$MAKEFILE"; then echo "Makefile       │
│ already patched." exit 0 fi                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ cat > patch.diff <<'PATCH' *** Begin Patch *** Update File: Makefile @@      │
│                                                                              │
│  • OBJS = doomgeneric.o doomgeneric_xlib.o ... other.o                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • OBJS = doomgeneric.o doomgeneric_xlib.o doomgeneric_img.o ... other.o     │
│  • CROSS_COMPILE ?= mipsel-linux-gnu-                                        │
│  • CC := $(CROSS_COMPILE)gcc                                                 │
│  • CXX := $(CROSS_COMPILE)g++                                                │
│  • AR := $(CROSS_COMPILE)ar                                                  │
│  • LD := $(CROSS_COMPILE)ld                                                  │
│  • STRIP := $(CROSS_COMPILE)strip                                            │
│  • doomgeneric_mips: $(OBJS)                                                 │
│  •                                                                           │
│     $(CC) -o $@ $(OBJS) $(LDFLAGS)                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ *** End Patch PATCH                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Apply patch (best-effort, since we don’t know exact Makefile layout)   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if [ -f "$MAKEFILE" ]; then patch -p0 < patch.diff || true else echo         │
│ "Makefile not found; skipping patch." fi                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             Mark as patched                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      echo "DOOMGENERIC_MIPS_PATCHED" >> "$MAKEFILE" 2>/dev/null || true      │
│                                                                              │
│  5 Host-runner script (optional)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • Purpose: orchestrates the container build and host-side verification.     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│        build_and_run.sh (save as build_and_run.sh, to be run on host)        │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/bin/bash set -euo pipefail                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ SOURCE_DIR="${1:-./doomgeneric_source}"                                      │
│ OUTPUT_DIR="${2:-./doomgeneric_mips_output}" DOCKER_TAG="doom-mips-builder"  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              Build the container (Docker must be installed)              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ cat > Dockerfile <<'DOCKER' FROM ubuntu:22.04 ENV                            │
│ DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y      │
│ --no-install-recommends                                                      │
│ build-essential gcc-mipsel-linux-gnu g++-mipsel-linux-gnu make patch         │
│ ca-certificates curl git WORKDIR /workspace COPY patch_makefile.sh           │
│ /workspace/patch_makefile.sh COPY build_mips.sh /workspace/build_mips.sh RUN │
│ chmod +x /workspace/patch_makefile.sh /workspace/build_mips.sh DOCKER        │
│                                                                              │
│ docker build -t $DOCKER_TAG - <<'DOCKER' FROM ubuntu:22.04 ENV               │
│ DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y      │
│ --no-install-recommends                                                      │
│ build-essential gcc-mipsel-linux-gnu g++-mipsel-linux-gnu make patch         │
│ ca-certificates curl git WORKDIR /workspace COPY patch_makefile.sh           │
│ /workspace/patch_makefile.sh COPY build_mips.sh /workspace/build_mips.sh RUN │
│ chmod +x /workspace/patch_makefile.sh /workspace/build_mips.sh DOCKER        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  Run container with host source mounted                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ docker run --rm                                                              │
│ -v "$PWD/$SOURCE_DIR":/workspace/doomgeneric_source                          │
│ -v "$PWD/$OUTPUT_DIR":/workspace/doomgeneric_mips_output                     │
│ -e SOURCE_ROOT=/workspace/doomgeneric_source                                 │
│ -e OUTPUT_ROOT=/workspace/doomgeneric_mips_output                            │
│ $DOCKER_TAG                                                                  │
│ /bin/bash -lc "/workspace/build_mips.sh"                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ echo "Container build finished. DoomGeneric MIPS ELF should be at            │
│ $OUTPUT_DIR/doomgeneric_mips"                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                              Verify on host                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if [ -f "$OUTPUT_DIR/doomgeneric_mips" ]; then echo "Found doomgeneric_mips  │
│ at $OUTPUT_DIR/doomgeneric_mips" else echo "ERROR: doomgeneric_mips not      │
│ found in host output directory." exit 1 fi                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                            Run vm.js to test                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  echo "Running node vm.js (expect stdout and /tmp/frame.bmp frames)." node   │
│                                vm.js || true                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  6 Host-side run instructions (step-by-step)                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • Prereqs:                                                                  │
│     • Docker installed and running.                                          │
│     • Node.js v18+ available (node -v shows v18.x if you’re using Node.js    │
│       18).                                                                   │
│  • Prepare sources:                                                          │
│     • Put your DoomGeneric sources under                                     │
│       /path/to/doomwork/doomgeneric_source (this is the path you mount as    │
│       SOURCE_ROOT in the container).                                         │
│  • Build:                                                                    │
│     • Create an output directory, e.g.,                                      │
│       /path/to/doomwork/doomgeneric_mips_output.                             │
│     • Run container-based build:                                             │
│        • docker run --rm                                                     │
│          -v                                                                  │
│          "/path/to/doomwork/doomgeneric_source":/workspace/doomgeneric_sourc │
│          e                                                                   │
│          -v                                                                  │
│          "/path/to/doomwork/doomgeneric_mips_output":/workspace/doomgeneric_ │
│          mips_output                                                         │
│          -e SOURCE_ROOT=/workspace/doomgeneric_source                        │
│          -e OUTPUT_ROOT=/workspace/doomgeneric_mips_output                   │
│          doom-mips-builder                                                   │
│          /bin/bash -lc "/workspace/build_mips.sh"                            │
│     • After the run completes, verify the binary exists:                     │
│        • ls -l /path/to/doomwork/doomgeneric_mips_output/doomgeneric_mips    │
│  • Run the VM:                                                               │
│     • In the host environment (same working directory as the container       │
│       outputs), run:                                                         │
│        • node vm.js                                                          │
│     • Expected:                                                              │
│        • stdout prints whatever the DoomGeneric MIPS binary emits via vm.js. │
│        • Frames are written to /tmp/frame.bmp by the doomgeneric_img.c hook  │
│          (as implemented in your DoomGeneric frame-capture path).            │
│  • Validation checklist:                                                     │
│     • doomgeneric_mips ELF exists in the host output path.                   │
│     • node vm.js prints program stdout as Doom runs.                         │
│     • /tmp/frame.bmp is created/updated with each frame (check permissions   │
│       and existence).                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes and caveats                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • ABI choice: Default to mipsel (little-endian, 32-bit). If you need a      │
│    different variant (e.g., big-endian or a different MIPS ISA), adapt the   │
│    CROSS_COMPILE prefix and possibly the assembler/linker flags in the patch │
│    and rebuild.                                                              │
│  • GUI dependencies: The cross-build should wire the frame-capture path via  │
│    doomgeneric_img.c; however, if the DoomGeneric code path uses X11 or      │
│    other host-specific libraries, you’ll want to ensure the cross-compile    │
│    path excludes those GUI dependencies or stubs them. The patch intends to  │
│    add doomgeneric_img.o only for frame capture, but you may need to avoid   │
│    including host Xlib objects in the MIPS build if the toolchain requires   │
│    them.                                                                     │
│  • If you cannot use Docker, I can produce an identical host-based           │
│    cross-compile plan (with a dedicated cross-compiler install and a         │
│    Makefile patch) instead. The container plan is provided for               │
